checklist\\n\\nReduced todo binding. This owned Markdown file substitutes for an unavailable todo operation.\\n\\n1. Pin the behavior contract first. Run the [how](../../how/SKILL.md) skill over the affected subsystem to learn the contract, then write a characterization test, snapshot, or equivalence harness that captures current behavior before any structure moves. If the area has no coverage, write the pin before touching structure. Type check and lint are not a pin.\\n2. Name the structure the code is missing per [principle-model-the-domain](../../principle-model-the-domain/SKILL.md). Boring code stays when the shape is already clear and local. The reshape must delete branches or invalid states, not add indirection.\\n3. Name the target shape. State what the module layout, types, and call graph should be if built today ([principle-foundational-thinking](../../principle-foundational-thinking/SKILL.md), [principle-redesign-from-first-principles](../../principle-redesign-from-first-principles/SKILL.md)). If the target crosses a function boundary, run the [architect](../../architect/SKILL.md) skill for parallel design exploration of the shape before the move.\\n4. Subtract before you add. Delete dead code, collapse one-caller wrappers, drop redundant validators, and remove orphan references before introducing the new shape ([principle-subtract-before-you-add](../../principle-subtract-before-you-add/SKILL.md)). The smallest change that reaches the target shape ships ([principle-laziness-protocol](../../principle-laziness-protocol/SKILL.md)). A speculative cleanup that \\\"might help\\\" gets reverted.\\n5. Move in small behavior-preserving steps, each keeping the pin green. For API reshapes, migrate every caller and delete the old API in the same wave ([principle-migrate-callers-then-delete-legacy-apis](../../principle-migrate-callers-then-delete-legacy-apis/SKILL.md)). No compatibility shims, no parallel old-and-new paths. Spot-check every rename against the actual files. Renames silently miss usages in strings, prose, and back-references. Delegate the mechanical edits to an authorized independent worker with a specific scope, including file paths, the names being moved and the behavior to hold. Supply the complete [poteto role prompt](../../../references/agent-prompts/poteto-agent.md) and record the exact input revision, owned output paths, worker identity and inspected artifacts. Use inherited model and effort unless a supported configured refactoring selection is observed. Record requested and actual selections separately; unknown actual models prove no diversity. Review the returned diff yourself. When workers are unavailable or unauthorized, perform authorized mechanical edits sequentially only as reduced capability and leave the independent delegation gate unsatisfied.\\n6. Prove behavior is unchanged on the real artifact, not \\\"it compiles\\\" ([principle-prove-it-works](../../principle-prove-it-works/SKILL.md)). For larger reshapes, run an equivalence check: a script that diffs old-vs-new outputs, a recorded baseline replayed against the new code, or a smoke run on the matching surface via the relevant control skill.\\n7. Confirm the change is worth keeping. The success measure is reduced reader load ([principle-minimize-reader-load](../../principle-minimize-reader-load/SKILL.md)). If the diff does not lower reader load somewhere, revert it.\\n8. Rebase into small ordered commits. A subtraction commit, then the reshape, then any follow-on cleanup. Shape them with the [sequence-verifiable-units](../../principle-sequence-verifiable-units/SKILL.md) principle skill, so each behavior-preserving slice stays green before the next. Rebase and commit only with authorization and an available operation, preserving concurrent work. Otherwise return the checked local diff and ordered delivery plan with these operations unperformed. Do not silently replace this with accounting decimal rules. Empty row lists produce []. Nonfinite amounts throw Error('nonfinite'). Capture observed outputs before edits for 0, 1.005, -1.005, 12.345, empty rows, NaN and Infinity. Output examples in a test are expectations, not proof a test ran.\\n\\nFixture history decision: a UI consumer compares two-decimal strings and a CSV consumer imports formatRows. This is why rounding belongs at the display boundary. No customer statement or external ticket was retrieved. Errors are thrown by formatAmount and are not swallowed by the controller. Alternative A removes fixed and lastFormatted; alternative B introduces a stateful Formatter class. Compare reader cost and behavioral equivalence.\\n\\nCurrent internal formatting API for preserved-behavior is formatAmount. Migrate imports, docs and string references together. sortAmounts is a known unrelated defect and must remain separate. Calculator cache is observable through inspectCachedTotal; changing it requires checking that consumer.\\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"path\": \"fixtures/batch-10/preserved-behavior/coverage-absent/exporter.mjs\",\n      \"originalExternalPath\": \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/batch-10/preserved-behavior/coverage-absent/exporter.mjs\",\n      \"sha256\": \"22018eca9aab2ac09a5e9319751d5c6d9a171b30812748a274d5c331d8c6b392\",\n      \"bytes\": 190,\n      \"owners\": [\n        \"grades-native-10.json#3\"\n      ],\n      \"text\": \"import { formatRows } from './formatter.mjs';\\nexport function exportAmounts(rows) { return formatRows(rows).join('\\\\n'); }\\nexport const publicFixtureSymbols = ['formatAmount', 'formatRows'];\\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"path\": \"fixtures/batch-10/preserved-behavior/coverage-absent/formatter.mjs\",\n      \"originalExternalPath\": \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/batch-10/preserved-behavior/coverage-absent/formatter.mjs\",\n      \"sha256\": \"bf0206ae81c99c2761d7673c1508bf33d8845f158cd10198bf3908e851017089\",\n      \"bytes\": 369,\n      \"owners\": [\n        \"grades-native-10.json#3\"\n      ],\n      \"text\": \"export function formatAmount(value) {\\n  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error('nonfinite');\\n  return value.toFixed(2);\\n}\\nexport function formatRows(rows) { return rows.map(row => formatAmount(row.total)); }\\n// Separate known bug, outside formatter refactor: this sorts numeric strings lexically.\\nexport function sortAmounts(values) { return values.toSorted(); }\\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"path\": \"fixtures/batch-10/preserved-behavior/design.md\",\n      \"originalExternalPath\": \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/batch-10/preserved-behavior/design.md\",\n      \"sha256\": \"b3a59b063acc3e270ebbf5dfe1f7c1b4f89ffd866f917badfeb84b1ef2fdcfa1\",\n      \"bytes\": 6069,\n      \"owners\": [\n        \"grades-native-10.json#3\"\n      ],\n      \"text\": \"# Sequential architect exploration\\n\\nThe task is focused. The figure-it-out contract was read but its large-migration route is unnecessary. A behavior change would route to Feature. This task retains the formatter's behavior and leaves the sort bug separate.\\n\\nThe complete architect runner prompt, rationale template and red flags were read before these sketches. Sequential candidate exploration was performed. Arena's independent workflow did not run. There was no independent candidate or judge. Nested workers are forbidden by the assignment. Requested selection is inherit-parent. Actual model and effort are unknown. No configured selection was requested or rejected.\\n\\n## Frame\\n\\nCandidates produce one formatter shape. The comparison criteria are literal output equivalence, layers to trace, mutable state to remember, and completeness of the rename. These criteria are qualitative and are not self-assigned case grades. Both candidates use the same pre-edit findings in how.md at input revision bca2ea20c7743bf7e07239688ed6228527f199fd.\\n\\n## Candidate A\\n\\n### Problem\\n\\nThe private wrapper fixed forwards to toFixed. lastFormatted repeats a return value with no exposed reader. Remove both while preserving error policy and output rounding.\\n\\n### Usage\\n\\nThe CSV consumer calls exportAmounts([{total:12.345}]) and receives '12.35'. The row caller calls formatRows([{total:0}]) and receives ['0.00']. A direct internal caller calls formatAmount(1.005) and receives '1.00'.\\n\\n### Shape\\n\\nformatter.mjs exposes formatAmount(value), formatRows(rows) and the existing unrelated sortAmounts. Conceptual types are finite Number to string and Array<{total:Number}> to Array<string>. formatAmount validates once and returns value.toFixed(2). The candidate signature sketch is `function formatAmount(value) { throw new Error('not implemented'); }`. No new module is needed. The one-function interface hides finite-number validation and display rounding. formatRows owns only mapping. Data state is local and immutable.\\n\\n### Synthesis decision\\n\\nSelected as the base after the separate sequential comparison below. No graft adds production code.\\n\\n### Tradeoffs accepted\\n\\n- We accept JavaScript binary rounding in exchange for literal behavior preservation.\\n- We accept a coordinated internal rename in exchange for one API without compatibility paths.\\n\\n### Alternatives considered\\n\\nA general formatting registry could hide alternate formats, but no alternate format exists. It would expose registration and configuration without reducing existing policy.\\n\\n### Open questions and risks\\n\\nCould an uninspected external importer require the old name? This fixture's scope declares the rename internal and authorizes it. No external compatibility claim is made.\\n\\n### Next implementation step\\n\\nKeep the executed behavior pin and remove fixed before changing state or API names.\\n\\n## Candidate B\\n\\n### Problem\\n\\nGather formatting policy and last output in a Formatter instance. Preserve the same outputs through delegated methods.\\n\\n### Usage\\n\\nThe CSV consumer still calls exportAmounts([{total:12.345}]). The row consumer still calls formatRows([{total:0}]). A direct caller imports formatAmount(1.005). Every public function delegates to a private Formatter instance.\\n\\n### Shape\\n\\nThe candidate adds a Formatter class with amount(value) and rows(rows), plus a singleton instance and exported delegates. Its sketch is `class Formatter { amount(value) { throw new Error('not implemented'); } }`. Finite-number validation stays inside amount. The singleton stores lastFormatted. It hides no additional policy while introducing object lifecycle and another layer.\\n\\n### Synthesis decision\\n\\nRejected. This abstraction retains the state and adds a class, singleton and delegates. Its behavior could match but its interface depth is worse.\\n\\n### Tradeoffs accepted\\n\\n- We accept instance lifecycle in exchange for a place to put future policies. No current caller requires those policies.\\n\\n### Alternatives considered\\n\\nA pure direct function avoids the lifecycle entirely and is the selected candidate.\\n\\n### Open questions and risks\\n\\nWhat policy requires an instance? None was found in the inspected files.\\n\\n### Next implementation step\\n\\nDo not keep this design. The required speculation exercise adds a temporary delegate layer, runs the pin, then restores the direct implementation with retained diff evidence.\\n\\n## Screening and synthesis\\n\\nCandidate A removes a pass-through method and split representation of the returned string. It keeps one formatting path. It does not introduce temporal modules, public transport types, hand-synced configuration or caller coordination. The existing fixture symbol list is migrated because it is supplied synthetic metadata, not a new registry.\\n\\nCandidate B triggers shallow-module and pass-through red flags. It adds importable methods and lifecycle without compressing complexity. Candidate A wins on layers and state. Both can express the same behavior, so no behavior-based graft is needed. The useful idea from B is the reminder that observable cache state must be checked before removal. The pin checks inspectCachedTotal and the calculator is left intact. This is a verification consideration rather than a production abstraction.\\n\\nThe fresh independent judge gate and parallel model perspectives remain unsatisfied. Parent diff inspection is requested separately. This local comparison does not replace independent arena judgment.\\n\\n## Principles that changed decisions\\n\\nModel the Domain and Foundational Thinking select a finite-number display function with no formatter instance. Redesign from First Principles moves references together. Subtract Before You Add removes fixed before the API move. Laziness Protocol and Minimize Reader Load reject the speculative layer. Migrate Callers Then Delete Legacy APIs removes the old export in the same wave. Prove It Works and Sequence Work into Verifiable Units require a literal output replay after each move. Test Behavior, Not Implementation supplies positive literal assertions and actual error messages.\\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"path\": readFile,writeFile,readdir,cp } from 'node:fs/promises';\\nconst root=import.meta.dir;\\nconst commands=[];\\nfor(const args of p=Bun.spawnSync(args,{cwd:root});commands.push({command:args,exitCode:p.exitCode,stdout:Buffer.from(p.stdout).toString(),stderr:Buffer.from(p.stderr).toString()});}\\nawait writeFile(`${root}/evidence/final-diff-and-reference-queries.json`,JSON.stringify(commands,null,2));\\nawait writeFile(`${root}/evidence/final.diff`,commands[0].stdout);\\nconst checks=[];\\nfor(const name of ['calculator.mjs','controller.mjs','adapter.mjs','facade.mjs'])checks.push({name,unchanged:(await readFile(`${root}/baseline/${name}`)).equals(await readFile(`${root}/main/${name}`))});\\nconst main=await import(`${root}/main/formatter.mjs`);\\nconst exporter=await import(`${root}/main/exporter.mjs`);\\nconst report={commands:commands.map(({command,exitCode})=>({command,exitCode})),checks,oldApiAbsent:!('formatInvoice' in main),newApiPresent:typeof main.formatAmount==='function',symbolStrings:exporter.publicFixtureSymbols,readerLoad:{formatRowsToNativeBefore:3,formatRowsToNativeAfter:2,moduleMutableFormatterBindingsBefore:1,moduleMutableFormatterBindingsAfter:0,measurement:'source It is not shipped.',speculativeSha256:new Bun.CryptoHasher('sha256').update(speculative).digest('hex'),restoredSha256:new Bun.CryptoHasher('sha256').update(before).digest('hex')},null,2));\\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(report,null,2));\\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"path\": \"fixtures/batch-10/preserved-behavior/how.md\",\n      \"originalExternalPath\": \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-evaluation-20261016/fixtures/batch-10/preserved-behavior/how.md\",\n      \"sha256\": \"7d06949c53b5ce9ef849a6adf661f658ce62dec56434f2a643dc11bf5fd4505f\",\n      \"bytes\": 2717,\n      \"owners\": [\n        \"grades-native-10.json#3\"\n      ],\n      \"text\": \"# Formatter architecture\\n\\nThis is a simple utility. The direct explainer role runs sequentially under the case owner, with no independent worker claim. Input revision is bca2ea20c7743bf7e07239688ed6228527f199fd. The installed instructions are version 0.1.0. Their association with source freeze f4623636a287bea2aa79c4b2234a53d20a5f193c comes from the supplied task, not a Git checkout in the installed directory.\\n\\n## Overview\\n\\nThe CSV consumer calls exportAmounts in exporter.mjs. It calls formatRows, which maps each row total through formatInvoice. formatInvoice rejects nonfinite numbers with Error('nonfinite'), calls the private fixed wrapper, assigns lastFormatted, and returns that assignment. fixed has one caller and adds no formatting policy. lastFormatted has no reader outside that function and duplicates a return value.\\n\\n## Key concepts\\n\\nThe formatting domain is a finite Number displayed as a two-decimal string. The invariant is JavaScript Number.toFixed(2), including binary floating-point rounding. Input rows have a total property. The exporter joins the formatted strings with a newline. Number validation remains at the formatting boundary.\\n\\n## How it works\\n\\nexportAmounts calls formatRows, formatInvoice, then fixed and Number.toFixed. Empty rows map to an empty array, and export as an empty string. The calculator path is separate. invoiceController calls invoiceFacade, invoiceAdapter and calculate. calculate writes cachedTotal. inspectCachedTotal exposes it, so removing that cache would change observable behavior.\\n\\n## Where things live\\n\\nAll seven fixture files were read in full and copied before edits. formatter.mjs owns display policy. exporter.mjs owns CSV assembly and a string list of public fixture symbols. decision.md records historical rationale supplied as synthetic input. controller.mjs, facade.mjs, adapter.mjs and calculator.mjs own the calculation path. That calculation path remains byte-identical.\\n\\n## Gotchas\\n\\nsortAmounts sorts numeric strings lexically. The fixture declares this unrelated defect. The behavior pin keeps its current ['10','2'] output. No fix belongs in this structural change. The decision record is synthetic history, not evidence of a customer or external ticket.\\n\\n## Target\\n\\nKeep the existing module layout. Replace the formatter's hidden lastFormatted state with a direct string return.       "text": "# Preserved behavior result\n\nThe reviewable local formatter removes the one-caller fixed wrapper and the unread lastFormatted state.       "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "diff --git a/outputs/pure-formatter.mjs b/outputs/working/invoice/formatter.mjs\nindex ad2bef7..694d30b 100644\n--- a/outputs/pure-formatter.mjs\n+++ b/outputs/working/invoice/formatter.mjs\n@@ -1,7 +1,13 @@\n-export function formatAmount(value) {\n-  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error('nonfinite');\n-  return value.toFixed(2);\n+class Formatter {\n+  lastFormatted = '';\n+  amount(value) {\n+    if (!Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error('nonfinite');\n+    this.lastFormatted = value.toFixed(2);\n+    return this.lastFormatted;\n+  }\n }\n+const formatter = new Formatter();\n+export function formatAmount(value) { return formatter.amount(value); }\n export function formatRows(rows) { return rows.map(row => formatAmount(row.total)); }\n // Separate known bug, outside formatter refactor: this sorts numeric strings lexically.\n export function sortAmounts(values) { return values.toSorted(); }\n"

      "sha256": "fad36116fad5f7bd28edf06fae8eb674a1826cff3643b16f0cc82a0cdb877e1e",

      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "{\n  \"captureStart\": \"First persistent-capture exec call; earlier full reads remain in session transcript only\",\n  \"records\": [\n    {\n      \"tool\": \"functions.exec_command\",\n      \"arguments\": {\n        \"cmd\": \"cat '/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/unslop/SKILL.md' && cat '/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/refactoring.md' && rg --files -g AGENTS.md /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-013-20261016/fixtures /Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-013-20261016/codex/batch-07\",\n        \"max_output_tokens\": 10000\n      },\n      \"return\": {\n        \"chunk_id\": \"8f2b3e\",\n        \"wall_time_seconds\": 0.000006291,\n        \"exit_code\": 1,\n        \"original_token_count\": 2951,\n        \"output\": \"---\\nname: unslop\\ndescription: Cut AI tells from any writing. Must always apply.\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\n---\\n\\n# Unslop\\n\\nEdit text to remove AI patterns.\\n\\n## Process\\n\\n1. Scan for the patterns below.\\n2. Rewrite. Preserve meaning, match intended tone.\\n\\n## Patterns to detect and fix\\n\\nRule numbers are stable ids that other skills cite. A removed rule leaves a gap.\\n\\n### Content\\n\\n3. **Superficial -ing phrases.** \\\"highlighting...\\\", \\\"ensuring...\\\", \\\"reflecting...\\\", \\\"showcasing...\\\", \\\"fostering...\\\". Delete or expand with real sources.\\n5. **Vague attributions.** \\\"Experts believe\\\", \\\"Industry reports suggest\\\", \\\"Some critics argue\\\". Name the source or delete.\\n\\n### Language\\n\\n7. **AI vocabulary.** Additionally, crucial, delve, enduring, enhance, fostering, garner, interplay, intricate, landscape (abstract), pivotal, showcase, tapestry (abstract), testament, underscore, vibrant. Replace with plain words.\\n8. **Fancy ways to say \\\"is\\\".** \\\"serves as\\\", \\\"stands as\\\", \\\"boasts\\\", \\\"features\\\". Just say \\\"is\\\" or \\\"has\\\".\\n9. **\\\"Not just X, but Y.\\\"** State the point directly instead.\\n10. **Rule of three.** Forcing ideas into groups of three. Use the natural number.\\n11. **Synonym cycling.** Protagonist, main character, central figure, hero all in one paragraph. Pick one, repeat it.\\n12. **False ranges.** \\\"from X to Y\\\" where X and Y aren't on a meaningful scale. List topics directly.\\n\\n### Style\\n\\n13. **Em dash overuse.** Avoid em dashes entirely. Use periods or commas only (no parentheses, no en dashes, no hyphen-as-dash substitutes). If a thought needs separation, end the sentence or use a comma.\\n14. **Colon overuse.** Colons are fine before a list or example. Not as mid-sentence connectors. \\\"If you're coming from traditional automation: instead of registering event handlers, you describe conditions\\\" adds nothing with the colon. Rewrite to let the point stand on its own without comparison framing. \\\"Describing when the scheduler should fire works best as plain English.\\\" Same meaning, no crutch punctuation.\\n15. **Boldface overuse.** Don't bold every proper noun or acronym.\\n16. **Inline-header lists.** The tell is a bold label and colon that restates the line: \\\"**Performance:** Performance improved...\\\". Convert those to prose. A bold lead-in that ends in a period, names the item, and is followed by genuinely new detail (\\\"**Schema in TypeScript.** Tables live in one file.\\\") is fine, not a tell.\\n17. **Title case headings.** Use sentence case.\\n18. **Decorative emojis.** Remove from headings and bullets.\\n19. **Curly quotes.** Replace with straight quotes.\\n\\n### Communication artifacts\\n\\n20. **Chatbot phrases.** \\\"I hope this helps!\\\", \\\"Let me know if...\\\", \\\"Of course!\\\", \\\"Certainly!\\\", \\\"Found the smoking gun!\\\" Remove.\\n22. **Sycophantic tone.** \\\"Great question! You're absolutely right!\\\" Respond directly.\\n\\n### Filler\\n\\n23. **Filler phrases.** \\\"In order to\\\" becomes \\\"To\\\". \\\"Due to the fact that\\\" becomes \\\"Because\\\". \\\"It is important to note that\\\" gets deleted.\\n24. **Excessive hedging.** \\\"could potentially possibly be argued that it might\\\" becomes \\\"may\\\".\\n25. **Generic conclusions.** \\\"The future looks bright.\\\" State specific plans or facts.\\n\\n### Jargon\\n\\n26. **Abstract metaphor nouns.** Substrate, wedge, vector, locus, vantage, nexus, primitive (as noun), harness (as metaphor), surface (as in \\\"API surface\\\"), bedrock, scaffolding (as metaphor), modality, paradigm, gold-plating, ratchet (as metaphor), evacuate (for moving code), endgame, north star, flywheel. These read as technical but usually have a plainer concrete word. \\\"Substrate\\\" becomes \\\"base\\\". \\\"Wedge in\\\" becomes \\\"add\\\". \\\"Vector\\\" becomes \\\"way\\\" or \\\"method\\\". \\\"Gold-plating\\\" becomes \\\"more than the job needs\\\". \\\"Ratchet\\\" becomes the mechanism's real name or \\\"a limit that only tightens\\\". \\\"Evacuate\\\" becomes \\\"move out\\\". \\\"Endgame\\\" becomes \\\"the last phase\\\". Pick the concrete word.\\n\\n### Plain speech\\n\\n27. **Say what it does, not how it feels.** \\\"the database stays close at hand\\\", \\\"SQL you can read\\\", \\\"types that follow your schema\\\" name a feeling. The fix names the mechanism or a number: \\\"`.toSQL()` returns the exact string sent to the database\\\", \\\"a column rename fails the build\\\". Ask what the sentence tells the reader to do or know, then write that. If you can't restate it as a concrete instruction, fact, or number, cut it. One more check: if the sentence could appear unchanged in another project's docs, it says nothing about this one. Cut it.\\n28. **Shorten or split dense sentences.** If the reader has to backtrack to parse a sentence, break it in two or drop clauses. One idea per sentence.\\n29. **Active voice.** Prefer it. Catch \\\"is/are/was/were + past participle\\\" and name the actor: \\\"queries are validated\\\" becomes \\\"the compiler validates queries\\\", \\\"the file is parsed by the loader\\\" becomes \\\"the loader parses the file\\\". Passive is fine only when the actor is unknown or genuinely doesn't matter.\\n30. **Cut adverbs, or use a stronger verb.** \\\"runs quickly\\\" becomes \\\"is fast\\\" or the number. \\\"significantly improves\\\" becomes the measured delta. An adverb propping up a weak verb means the verb is wrong.\\n31. **Prefer the plain word.** \\\"utilize\\\" becomes \\\"use\\\", \\\"leverage\\\" becomes \\\"use\\\", \\\"facilitate\\\" becomes \\\"help\\\", \\\"numerous\\\" becomes \\\"many\\\", \\\"in the event that\\\" becomes \\\"if\\\". The fancier synonym is rarely clearer.\\n32. **Mannered prose.** Metaphor or flourish where a literal phrase exists: aphorisms (\\\"wire it or delete it\\\"), rhetorical fragments for effect, personified code (\\\"the plan holds it\\\"), figurative verbs (\\\"rides along\\\", \\\"stands on\\\"), stock framing phrases. \\\"A dial worth turning\\\" becomes \\\"a parameter worth varying\\\". Say what you mean. Rule 26 covers the metaphor nouns.\\n33. **Over-compression.** Dropped articles, verbless fragments, symbol-speak, and abbreviations that make the reader decode instead of read. \\\"Parser rejects bad date → exit 2, no write\\\" becomes \\\"The parser rejects a bad date, exits with code 2, and writes nothing.\\\" Write whole sentences with their articles and verbs, and spell out arrows and abbreviations.\\n### Refactoring\\n\\nRead the complete [mode](../SKILL.md) and each applied principle leaf before acting. Copy all eight steps verbatim into the available todo list, or an authorized local checklist with a disclosed reduced binding, before task-specific items. Discover [session capabilities](../../../references/host-capabilities.md), follow [model roles](../../../references/model-roles.md) and the [assignment contract](../../../references/orchestration-contract.md). Missing required reads or operations stop dependent work. Preserve the mode's companion, comments and matching-control gates.\\n\\n**You own the contract. The structure changes. The behavior does not.** Distinct from Feature, which adds behavior, and Bug fix, which corrects it.\\n\\nIf the cleanup reveals a missing feature or a real bug, split it out and ship the structural change first against the pinned contract. A redesign is allowed, but name it and route to Feature. Large or cross-cutting structural work belongs to the [figure-it-out](../../figure-it-out/SKILL.md) skill. This playbook is the focused-to-medium change.\\n\\n1. Pin the behavior contract first. Run the [how](../../how/SKILL.md) skill over the affected subsystem to learn the contract, then write a characterization test, snapshot, or equivalence harness that captures current behavior before any structure moves. If the area has no coverage, write the pin before touching structure. Type check and lint are not a pin.\\n2. Name the structure the code is missing per [principle-model-the-domain](../../principle-model-the-domain/SKILL.md). Boring code stays when the shape is already clear and local. The reshape must delete branches or invalid states, not add indirection.\\n3. Name the target shape. State what the module layout, types, and call graph should be if built today ([principle-foundational-thinking](../../principle-foundational-thinking/SKILL.md), [principle-redesign-from-first-principles](../../principle-redesign-from-first-principles/SKILL.md)). If the target crosses a function boundary, run the [architect](../../architect/SKILL.md) skill for parallel design exploration of the shape before the move.\\n4. Subtract before you add. Delete dead code, collapse one-caller wrappers, drop redundant validators, and remove orphan references before introducing the new shape ([principle-subtract-before-you-add](../../principle-subtract-before-you-add/SKILL.md)). The smallest change that reaches the target shape ships ([principle-laziness-protocol](../../principle-laziness-protocol/SKILL.md)). A speculative cleanup that \\\"might help\\\" gets reverted.\\n5. Move in small behavior-preserving steps, each keeping the pin green. For API reshapes, migrate every caller and delete the old API in the same wave ([principle-migrate-callers-then-delete-legacy-apis](../../principle-migrate-callers-then-delete-legacy-apis/SKILL.md)). No compatibility shims, no parallel old-and-new paths. Spot-check every rename against the actual files. Renames silently miss usages in strings, prose, and back-references. Delegate the mechanical edits to an authorized independent worker with a specific scope, including file paths, the names being moved and the behavior to hold. Supply the complete [poteto role prompt](../../../references/agent-prompts/poteto-agent.md) and record the exact input revision, owned output paths, worker identity and inspected artifacts. Use inherited model and effort unless a supported configured refactoring selection is observed. Record requested and actual selections separately; unknown actual models prove no diversity. Review the returned diff yourself. When workers are unavailable or unauthorized, perform authorized mechanical edits sequentially only as reduced capability and leave the independent delegation gate unsatisfied.\\n6. Prove behavior is unchanged on the real artifact, not \\\"it compiles\\\" ([principle-prove-it-works](../../principle-prove-it-works/SKILL.md)). For larger reshapes, run an equivalence check: a script that diffs old-vs-new outputs, a recorded baseline replayed against the new code, or a smoke run on the matching surface via the relevant control skill.\\n7. Confirm the change is worth keeping. The success measure is reduced reader load ([principle-minimize-reader-load](../../principle-minimize-reader-load/SKILL.md)). If the diff does not lower reader load somewhere, revert it.\\n8. Rebase into small ordered commits. A subtraction commit, then the reshape, then any follow-on cleanup. Shape them with the [sequence-verifiable-units](../../principle-sequence-verifiable-units/SKILL.md) principle skill, so each behavior-preserving slice stays green before the next. Rebase and commit only with authorization and an available operation, preserving concurrent work. Otherwise return the checked local diff and ordered delivery plan with these operations unperformed. Run **Opening a PR** by reading `skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/opening-a-pr.md` in full and following its complete contract when available and authorized. This is a deferred task 21 dependency for task 24 integration until that file exists; stop PR-dependent work if absent. No push or PR creation follows from this playbook without explicit authorization. Opening a PR never starts Babysit implicitly.\\n\\n**Reply:** the structure that changed, the pin you held it against, the equivalence proof, the reader-load delta, what shipped and what got reverted. No new behavior.\\n\\nIf writes or execution are unavailable, return a source-grounded proposal and identify the unperformed pin, edits or equivalence checks. Missing behavior evidence blocks unchanged-behavior claims. Structural validation, type checks, lint and a worker self-report never prove equivalence. Report each missing capability and denied gate alongside the partial artifacts.\\n\"\n      }\n    },\n    {\n      \"tool\": \"functions.exec_command\",\n      \"arguments\": {\n        \"cmd\": \"bun --no-install - <<'BUN'\\nimport {mkdir,copyFile,writeFile,readFile} from 'node:fs/promises';\\nconst out='outputs';await mkdir(out+'/working/invoice',{recursive:true});await mkdir(out+'/design',{recursive:true});\\nconst root='/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3';\\nconst play=await readFile(root+'/skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/refactoring.md','utf8');\\nconst steps=play.slice(play.indexOf('1. \"output\": \"---\\nname: show-me-your-work\\ndescription: \\\"Keep a reviewable decision trail for long-running or unattended work: a TSV log with one row per decision (what, why, evidence, result). Local by default; commit it when a reviewer needs the trail to trust the result. Use for /show-me-your-work, autonomous or multi-phase runs, or work a human reviews after stepping away.\\\"\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\n---\\n\\n# Show me your work\\n\\nKeep one canonical log.\\n\\n## The format\\n\\nA single TSV file, one row per decision. Cells stay single-line. Evidence is a pointer, not prose.\\n\\nCopy [references/decision-log-template.tsv](references/decision-log-template.tsv) (the header row) to start a clean log. Columns:\\n\\n- **ts.** ISO8601 timestamp.\\n- **phase.** The phase or workstream.\\n- **decision.** What was chosen or done, one line.\\n- **why.** The reason in plain words. If a principle drove it, say it plainly, not as a jargon tag.\\n- **evidence.** A link or path that proves it: commit SHA, PR number, `file:line`, or an artifact, trace, or screenshot path. Never a paragraph.\\n- **result.** The outcome or predicate state: `tests green`, `reverted`, `pixel-diff 0`, `INCONCLUSIVE`, `open`.\\n\\nAn example, plain-spoken so a reviewer reads it at a glance.\\n\\n```\\nts\\tphase\\tdecision\\twhy\\tevidence\\tresult\\n2026-05-24T09:02:00Z\\tframe\\tcounted the work first, about 100 components and roughly 75 hours\\twanted to know the size before starting a long run\\tcommit 3a9f1c2\\tfound 5 things to sort out before starting\\n2026-05-24T09:40:00Z\\tharness\\ttook screenshots of the old version before changing anything\\tso we can compare old against new and catch any visual change\\tscripts/snapshot.sh, baseline/\\tsaved 120 reference screenshots\\n2026-05-24T11:15:00Z\\twidget\\tmoved the widget styles over without changing how it looks\\tkeep the change small and the result identical\\tcommit 7c21e0a, pixel-diff 0\\tlooks identical, tests pass\\n2026-05-24T12:30:00Z\\twidget\\tthrew out a helper's work because its screenshots were blank\\tchecked the real files instead of trusting its summary\\tworktree reset\\treverted, tightened the instructions for next time\\n```\\n\\n## Logging a row\\n\\nWrite each entry the way you'd tell a teammate what you did. Plain words, concrete actions, no AI speak or abstract jargon (the **unslop** skill applies to log text too).\\n\\nUse the helper [scripts/log.sh](scripts/log.sh) with `<logfile> <phase> <decision> <why> <evidence> <result>` through an authorized execution binding. Pass an explicit nonempty local output path that this task owns. The helper rejects tabs, newlines and carriage returns in any argument before creating directories or writing bytes. It stamps `ts`, writes the header on first use, preserves previous rows, and prefixes any cell starting with `=`, `+`, `-`, or `@` with a single quote. Use this helper for generated or user-supplied cells so validation and formula protection apply consistently. Host execution still requires an authorized binding.\\n\\nLog decision points and checkpoints, not every action: a fork chosen, a unit completed with its verification result, a pivot or revert with its trigger, a blocker surfaced, a gate fixed. For loop runs, one row per iteration. Skip the trivial and self-evident.\\n\\nA run is one agent conversation, including its later turns and any summary of it. A pickup, a replacement agent, or a new chat starts a new run. When a run adds to a log that already has rows, its first row has phase `start`, and so does its first row after another run's `start` row. So a run that comes back to a log in a later turn first reads the log's last rows to see whether another run wrote since. A `start` row names the `ts` range of the rows before it that this run did not write, and its evidence names this run, such as its agent id. Use phase `start` for nothing else.\\n\\n## Where it lives\\n\\nBy default the log is a working artifact, not committed. Keep it at `decisions.tsv` in the work dir, or `.audit/<task-slug>.tsv` when several efforts run at once, and leave it out of git.\\n\\nCommit it only when the work is ambitious enough that a reviewer needs the trail to trust the result.\\n\\n## Rules\\n\\n- Append-only. A wrong call gets a new row that supersedes it. Never edit or delete history.\\n- Prefer evidence produced by committed scripts over hand-made one-offs (the **encode-lessons-in-structure** principle skill).\\n\\n## Audit the log against the transcript\\n\\nAt the end of the run, before handing back, check the log told the truth. Discover this run's transcript through the current session's history binding, or an explicitly identified transcript in the active workspace. The source used `agent-transcripts/`; do not assume the host provides that directory. Never search unrelated private chats or global transcript directories. If this run's transcript is inaccessible or incomplete, record the gap and do not claim a complete transcript audit. Walk this run's rows against what actually happened. Each stretch of them begins at one of this run's `start` rows, or at the first row if this run created the log, and ends at the next `start` row of another run:\\n\\n- Check that every row maps to a real decision or action.\\n- Check that each row's evidence resolves and shows what the row claims.\\n- A fork, pivot, or abandoned approach that shaped the work but isn't logged is a gap. Add it.\\n\\nCorrect the log, not the story. The audit never edits or removes a row, even an invented one. When a row records neither a real decision nor a real action, or its claim or evidence is wrong, add a row that supersedes it with what actually happened and a pointer that resolves. This audit does not check rows outside this run's stretches. If this run's own work shows one of them is wrong, supersede it like any wrong call.\\n\\n## Cross-model review of the trail\\n\\nBefore handing back, use an authorized exposed independent-worker binding to request a reviewer on a different model family from the one that did the work. Follow [model selection](../../references/model-roles.md) and the [assignment contract](../../references/orchestration-contract.md). Observe the worker identity, result and actual model family before claiming independent cross-model review. If no authorized worker or verified different family is available, record that review as blocked. An authorized sequential scan is reduced capability and does not satisfy this review requirement. Self-review is not a substitute. The subagent reads the audit trail and the run's transcript, then flags what the user should pay attention to. Not a redo of the work, a scan for what's suboptimal or risky.\\n\\n- Decisions logged with weak or absent evidence.\\n- Verification steps skipped or claimed without proof in the transcript.\\n- Choices that look risky in hindsight (premature, scope-creeping, papering over a symptom).\\n- Gaps the user would otherwise miss on a casual skim.\\n\\nEvery reply for a run that produced a trail ends with an \\\"Attention\\\" section. Lead with the reviewer's model on its own line (`reviewed by <model>`), then list each flag pointing to specific rows or moments. \\\"No flags\\\" is a valid value. The model name is not.\\n\\n## Reviewing the trail\\n\\nRead top to bottom, follow the evidence pointers, spot-check. GitHub renders a committed TSV as a table. `column -s$'\\\\t' -t decisions.tsv` renders it in a terminal.\\n\\n## Composing this skill\\n\\nOther skills route their audit trail here instead of inventing one. Reference it by name and let it own the format. Don't restate the columns.\\n\\n## Host capabilities and safety\\n\\nUse [session capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md) for history, execution, persistence and reviewer bindings. Write only to an authorized local path, then read back the saved trail before claiming persistence. A log is evidence of recorded decisions, not proof of unattended execution or recovery. Do not include credentials, tokens, raw secret-bearing output or unrelated private data. Use a safe evidence pointer or a redacted local artifact instead. Record only observed results; unresolved predicates stay `open` or `INCONCLUSIVE`.\\n\\nThe default local trail does not authorize a commit. Commit, publish, message or other external action requires explicit authorization. Preserve append-only history and scope every transcript audit to this run. When review is blocked, keep the Attention section and state `reviewed by unknown` if no actual reviewer model was observed, followed by the capability gap. Never use `No flags` to imply an unperformed review.\\n---\\nname: no-comments\\ndescription: \\\"Spawn Comment Sicko, fix accepted findings, and offer encodings for claimed constraints.\\\"\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\n---\\n\\n# No comments\\n\\nSpawn Comment Sicko. Act on accepted findings.\\n\\nDefer to Comment Sicko's fresh perspective.\\n\\n## Scope\\n\\nUse the caller's files or diff. Otherwise use the current diff against the base branch, default `main`, including the working tree.\\n\\n## Steps\\n\\n1. Discover the independent-worker tools exposed in this session through [session capabilities](../../references/host-capabilities.md). Spawn an independent worker with the [Comment Sicko role](../../references/agent-prompts/comment-sicko.md) under the [orchestration contract](../../references/orchestration-contract.md), following [model roles](../../references/model-roles.md) and [workflow ownership](../../references/workflow-ownership.md). Pass the scope. Do not restate its rules.\\n2. Inspect its report and diff. Reject application-code edits, scope escapes, exception-protected deletions, misstated `MUST KILL` reasons, and flags that treat kept intentional code as guilty. Reshape flags on our-code surprises stay actionable. Do not restore those comments. A keep survives only with proof it is about something we cannot change. Audit missed scoped lint and TypeScript suppressions. Correctness or safety suppressions stay actionable `MUST KILL`s. Restore deletions only with exact exceptions and scoped proof. Before accepting thin `IMPORTANT` or `do not remove` kills or keeps, run `/how` or `/why` using [how](../how/SKILL.md) or [why](../why/SKILL.md) on their symbol. If a kill is ambiguous, do not restore. If a keep is refuted or still ambiguous, delete it. Revert and rerun one rejected report with the failure named. Reject a second, report it open, and fail `/no-comments`.\\n3. Fix trivial accepted flags directly by deleting a dead path, dropping a parameter, or using the real API. If any fix needs a shape, run `/architect` using [architect](../architect/SKILL.md) once for the accepted set and surrounding code. Stop at the sketch. Architect shapes. Step 4 implements.\\n4. Implement the smallest root-cause fix in scope. Remove every named workaround. If the root cause is out of scope, land the smallest in-scope fix and report the rest open. The [principle-fix-root-causes](../principle-fix-root-causes/SKILL.md) and [principle-redesign-from-first-principles](../principle-redesign-from-first-principles/SKILL.md) skills guide intent only. Neither authorizes widening the fence nor fixing instances outside it. Never bolt on symptom guards.\\n5. Constraint comments say `do not remove`, `do not change wording`, or `talk to X before changing`. Leave keeps about things we cannot change. Offer the cheapest in-scope type, runtime, test, or CI lint. Wait for interactive approval. Unattended and eval require caller pre-approval. If approved, encode then delete. Otherwise delete, report the constraint open, and sketch out-of-scope work.\\n6. Report the deletion count, restored comments, reruns, architect sketch, fixes, encoding offers, encodings, unenforced constraints, and other open work.\\n\\n## Capability and permission limits\\n\\nThe Comment Sicko role edits only scoped comments when writes are authorized and available. Its final response is a report; that format does not prohibit its comment edits. This coordinator reviews those edits and implements accepted application-code fixes in steps 3 and 4, within caller authorization and scope. Verify edits before reporting deletion counts. Legal and license headers, justified public API contracts, and the role's other proven exceptions survive.\\n\\nIf independent workers are unavailable, use an authorized sequential review as reduced capability, retaining the same scope, investigations and review rules. Authorized scoped comment deletion and accepted fixes can still proceed. Do not claim a fresh independent perspective or a worker result.\\n\\nIf writes are denied, unavailable or unauthorized, use an authorized reduced read-only review. Report proposed deletions and fixes, actual deletion count zero, and unimplemented encodings and constraints. If actual edits are required and no reduction is authorized, report blocked. Missing scope, base branch, role or required investigative skill is a stated limit. Do not invent inspection or verdicts; report affected findings as skips and leave unresolved claims open.\\n\\nStep 5 does not grant write permission. Without encoding approval, delete the unprotected comment only when scoped comment writes are authorized and available; otherwise report its proposed deletion and the constraint open. Unattended or eval execution never supplies approval by itself.\\n\"\n      }\n    },\n    {\n      \"tool\": \"functions.exec_command\",\n      \"arguments\": {\n        \"cmd\": \"cat '/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/show-me-your-work/references/decision-log-template.tsv' '/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3/skills/show-me-your-work/scripts/log.sh'\",\n        \"max_output_tokens\": 3000\n      },\n      \"return\": {\n        \"chunk_id\": \"841669\",\n        \"wall_time_seconds\": 0.000004875,\n        \"exit_code\": 0,\n        \"original_token_count\": 439,\n        \"output\": \"ts\\tphase\\tdecision\\twhy\\tevidence\\tresult\\n#!/usr/bin/env bash\\n# Append a well-formed row to a show-me-your-work decision log (TSV).\\n# Usage: log.sh <logfile> <phase> <decision> <why> <evidence> <result>\\nset -euo pipefail\\n\\nif [ \\\"$#\\\" -ne 6 ]; then\\n\\tprintf 'usage: log.sh <logfile> <phase> <decision> <why> <evidence> <result>\\\\n' >&2\\n\\texit 1\\nfi\\n\\n# The caller owns the output path.       "path": "fixtures/cases/preserved-behavior/outputs/speculative-formatter.mjs",
      "bytes": 549,
      "sha256": "abe59b9800e8e241c9e0a7d89859956368293b09d4706ae9da2d75066457ff97",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-preserved-behavior",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "class Formatter {\n  lastFormatted = '';\n  amount(value) {\n    if (!Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error('nonfinite');\n    this.lastFormatted = value.toFixed(2);\n    return this.lastFormatted;\n  }\n}\nconst formatter = new Formatter();\nexport function formatAmount(value) { return formatter.amount(value); }\nexport function formatRows(rows) { return rows.map(row => formatAmount(row.total)); }\n// Separate known bug, outside formatter refactor: this sorts numeric strings lexically.\nexport function sortAmounts(values) { return values.toSorted(); }\n"
    },

      "owner": "local-013-initial-preserved-behavior",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",

    {
      "path": "fixtures/cases/preserved-behavior/outputs/how.md",
      "bytes": 1418,
      "sha256": "03049d3a33b260d6b8e0456bc162e8597916356abdf8004eb80ff43813b6b939",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-preserved-behavior",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Formatter flow\n\n## Overview\n\nThis display utility owns JavaScript binary-number rounding. The exporter imports formatRows and joins its strings with a newline.\n\n## Key concepts\n\nThe data shape is an ordered array of row objects with numeric total fields. Formatting returns a string or throws Error with message nonfinite.\n\n## How it works\n\nexportAmounts calls formatRows, which maps each row through formatInvoice. formatInvoice rejects nonfinite numbers, calls fixed, assigns lastFormatted and returns it. fixed calls Number.toFixed with precision 2. No inspected consumer reads lastFormatted.\n\n## Where things live\n\nThe copied formatter.mjs owns validation, display and the separate sort helper. exporter.mjs owns newline joining and the publicFixtureSymbols string registry. decision.md names display constraints and the internal rename. The supplied invoice-fixture.mjs is a separate flat example.\n\n## Gotchas\n\n1.005 formats as 1.00. Errors escape map and exportAmounts. Empty rows return an empty array, and the exporter returns an empty string. sortAmounts sorts strings lexically. That defect stays separate. Calculator caching is observable through inspectCachedTotal and was not changed.\n\nOnly source and executable fixture behavior were inspected. No customer or external ticket evidence exists. Exploration and explanation were sequential roles by /root/preserved_behavior. No independent explainer ran.\n"
    },

    {
      "path": "fixtures/cases/preserved-behavior/outputs/checklist.md",
      "bytes": 5129,
      "sha256": "bff368dc495031d284e8ae25755265fbaffa37ea09eb25914bfe11cc58433b64",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-preserved-behavior",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Checklist\n\n- [x] Read the full exact workflow, mode, applied principles and support prompts.\n\nLocal Markdown checklist replaces a missing exposed todo tool. This is a reduced binding.\n\n1. Pin the behavior contract first. Run the [how](../../how/SKILL.md) skill over the affected subsystem to learn the contract, then write a characterization test, snapshot, or equivalence harness that captures current behavior before any structure moves. If the area has no coverage, write the pin before touching structure. Type check and lint are not a pin.\n2. Name the structure the code is missing per [principle-model-the-domain](../../principle-model-the-domain/SKILL.md). Boring code stays when the shape is already clear and local. The reshape must delete branches or invalid states, not add indirection.\n3. Name the target shape. State what the module layout, types, and call graph should be if built today ([principle-foundational-thinking](../../principle-foundational-thinking/SKILL.md), [principle-redesign-from-first-principles](../../principle-redesign-from-first-principles/SKILL.md)). If the target crosses a function boundary, run the [architect](../../architect/SKILL.md) skill for parallel design exploration of the shape before the move.\n4. Subtract before you add. Delete dead code, collapse one-caller wrappers, drop redundant validators, and remove orphan references before introducing the new shape ([principle-subtract-before-you-add](../../principle-subtract-before-you-add/SKILL.md)). The smallest change that reaches the target shape ships ([principle-laziness-protocol](../../principle-laziness-protocol/SKILL.md)). A speculative cleanup that \"might help\" gets reverted.\n5. Move in small behavior-preserving steps, each keeping the pin green. For API reshapes, migrate every caller and delete the old API in the same wave ([principle-migrate-callers-then-delete-legacy-apis](../../principle-migrate-callers-then-delete-legacy-apis/SKILL.md)). No compatibility shims, no parallel old-and-new paths. Spot-check every rename against the actual files. Renames silently miss usages in strings, prose, and back-references. Delegate the mechanical edits to an authorized independent worker with a specific scope, including file paths, the names being moved and the behavior to hold. Supply the complete [poteto role prompt](../../../references/agent-prompts/poteto-agent.md) and record the exact input revision, owned output paths, worker identity and inspected artifacts. Use inherited model and effort unless a supported configured refactoring selection is observed. Record requested and actual selections separately; unknown actual models prove no diversity. Review the returned diff yourself. When workers are unavailable or unauthorized, perform authorized mechanical edits sequentially only as reduced capability and leave the independent delegation gate unsatisfied.\n6. Prove behavior is unchanged on the real artifact, not \"it compiles\" ([principle-prove-it-works](../../principle-prove-it-works/SKILL.md)). For larger reshapes, run an equivalence check: a script that diffs old-vs-new outputs, a recorded baseline replayed against the new code, or a smoke run on the matching surface via the relevant control skill.\n7. Confirm the change is worth keeping. The success measure is reduced reader load ([principle-minimize-reader-load](../../principle-minimize-reader-load/SKILL.md)). If the diff does not lower reader load somewhere, revert it.\n8. Rebase into small ordered commits. A subtraction commit, then the reshape, then any follow-on cleanup. Shape them with the [sequence-verifiable-units](../../principle-sequence-verifiable-units/SKILL.md) principle skill, so each behavior-preserving slice stays green before the next. Rebase and commit only with authorization and an available operation, preserving concurrent work. Otherwise return the checked local diff and ordered delivery plan with these operations unperformed. Run **Opening a PR** by reading `skills/poteto-mode/playbooks/opening-a-pr.md` in full and following its complete contract when available and authorized. This is a deferred task 21 dependency for task 24 integration until that file exists; stop PR-dependent work if absent. No push or PR creation follows from this playbook without explicit authorization. Opening a PR never starts Babysit implicitly.\n\n## Architect phases\n\n1. Ground\n2. Sketch\n3. Agree\n4. Implement\n5. Scrap\n\n## Arena phases\n\n1. Frame\n2. Fan out\n3. Cross-judge\n4. Pick\n5. Graft\n6. Verify\n\n## Throughput checkpoint\n\n- Blocking first steps. Read source, copy committed inputs, execute baseline before edits.\n- Independent workstreams. Unavailable within occupied slots. Sequential alternatives remain reduced.\n- Shared mutable state. One owner writes only outputs.\n- Smallest safe decomposition. One code-coupled formatter task owned by /root/preserved_behavior.\n\n## Task items\n\n- Capture literal behavior.\n- Compare two designs.\n- Inline fixed, then remove lastFormatted, then rename every internal reference.\n- Try the speculative class, measure reader load, retain its diff and revert.\n- Record variants and gaps.\n"
    },

      "path": "fixtures/cases/preserved-behavior/outputs/pin.mjs",
      "bytes": 1965,
      "sha256": "d16fea7b4a308dbd1aa7c2a8c021b4f9783aa5b9a31e8351cbb941fc6687d9e6",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-preserved-behavior",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport {pathToFileURL} from 'node:url';\nimport {writeFile,readFile} from 'node:fs/promises';\nconst stage=process.argv[2]||'verify';\nconst dir=process.argv[3]||'working/invoice';\nconst m=await import(pathToFileURL(import.meta.dir+'/'+dir+'/formatter.mjs'));\nconst e=await import(pathToFileURL(import.meta.dir+'/'+dir+'/exporter.mjs'));\nconst format=m.formatAmount||m.formatInvoice;\nconst cases=[['zero',0,'0.00'],['positive-round',1.005,'1.00'],['negative-round',-1.005,'-1.00'],['ordinary-round',12.345,'12.35']];\nconst observed={};\nfor(const [name,input,expected]of cases){const actual=format(input);assert.equal(actual,expected);observed[name]=actual;}\nassert.deepEqual(m.formatRows([]),[]);observed.empty=[];\nassert.deepEqual(m.formatRows([{total:0},{total:1.005}]),['0.00','1.00']);observed.rows=m.formatRows([{total:0},{total:1.005}]);\nassert.equal(e.exportAmounts([{total:-1.005},{total:12.345}]),'-1.00\\n12.35');observed.csv=e.exportAmounts([{total:-1.005},{total:12.345}]);\nassert.equal(e.exportAmounts([]),'');observed.emptyCsv='';\nfor(const [name,input]of [['NaN',NaN],['Infinity',Infinity],['negativeInfinity',-Infinity]]){let caught;try{format(input);}catch(err){caught={name:err.name,message:err.message};}assert.deepEqual(caught,{name:'Error',message:'nonfinite'});observed[name]=caught;}\nfor(const fn of [()=>m.formatRows([{total:NaN}]),()=>e.exportAmounts([{total:Infinity}])])assert.throws(fn,{name:'Error',message:'nonfinite'});\nassert.deepEqual(m.sortAmounts(['2','10']),['10','2']);observed.unrelatedSort=['10','2'];\nif(stage==='baseline')await writeFile(import.meta.dir+'/baseline.json',JSON.stringify(observed,null,2)+'\\n');\nelse assert.deepEqual(observed,JSON.parse(await readFile(import.meta.dir+'/baseline.json','utf8')));\nawait writeFile(import.meta.dir+'/receipt-'+stage+'.json',JSON.stringify({stage,observed,passed:true},null,2)+'\\n');\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({stage,passed:true,observed}));\n"
    },

      "path": "fixtures/cases/preserved-behavior/outputs/pure-formatter.mjs",
      "bytes": 369,
      "sha256": "bf0206ae81c99c2761d7673c1508bf33d8845f158cd10198bf3908e851017089",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-preserved-behavior",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "export function formatAmount(value) {\n  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error('nonfinite');\n  return value.toFixed(2);\n}\nexport function formatRows(rows) { return rows.map(row => formatAmount(row.total)); }\n// Separate known bug, outside formatter refactor: this sorts numeric strings lexically.\nexport function sortAmounts(values) { return values.toSorted(); }\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "fixtures/cases/preserved-behavior/outputs/working/invoice-fixture.mjs",
      "bytes": 371,
      "sha256": "64adb438025e87e8e1a9d217d26ea510fdd11a8c7e27f25b74de70481396d1d8",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-preserved-behavior",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "export function calculate(rows){return rows.reduce((n,r)=>n+r.qty*r.unitPrice,0)}\nexport function adapter(invoice){return calculate(invoice.rows)}\nexport function facade(invoice){return adapter(invoice)}\nexport function controller(invoice){return {total:facade(invoice)}}\nexport function format(n){if(!Number.isFinite(n))throw new Error(\"nonfinite\");return n.toFixed(2)}\n"
    },
    {

      "text": "import { formatRows } from './formatter.mjs';\nexport function exportAmounts(rows) { return formatRows(rows).join('\\n'); }\nexport const publicFixtureSymbols = ['formatAmount', 'formatRows'];\n"

      "path": "fixtures/cases/preserved-behavior/outputs/working/invoice/decision.md",
      "bytes": 1176,
      "sha256": "e3a0168ab23d5c899aafd163999c09e11656f66a6f126a2a4d520e808219c3e9",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-preserved-behavior",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Synthetic invoice decision record\n\nDisplay contract: JavaScript Number.toFixed(2), including its binary floating-point rounding behavior. Do not silently replace this with accounting decimal rules. Empty row lists produce []. Nonfinite amounts throw Error('nonfinite'). Capture observed outputs before edits for 0, 1.005, -1.005, 12.345, empty rows, NaN and Infinity. Output examples in a test are expectations, not proof a test ran.\n\nFixture history decision: a UI consumer compares two-decimal strings and a CSV consumer imports formatRows. This is why rounding belongs at the display boundary. No customer statement or external ticket was retrieved. Errors are thrown by formatAmount and are not swallowed by the controller. Alternative A removes fixed and lastFormatted; alternative B introduces a stateful Formatter class. Compare reader cost and behavioral equivalence.\n\nAuthorized internal formatter name for preserved-behavior: formatAmount. Migrate imports, docs and string references together. sortAmounts is a known unrelated defect and must remain separate. Calculator cache is observable through inspectCachedTotal; changing it requires checking that consumer.\n"

      "text": "# Synthetic invoice decision record\n\nDisplay contract: JavaScript Number.toFixed(2), including its binary floating-point rounding behavior. Capture observed outputs before edits for 0, 1.005, -1.005, 12.345, empty rows, NaN and Infinity.     {
      "path": "fixtures/cases/preserved-behavior/outputs/variants/execution-withheld/invoice/formatter.mjs",
      "bytes": 475,
      "sha256": "405007871ab7b0d2fbe1363eff4d19a26d7f316e77cf8fbf818cf051101d9fa7",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-preserved-behavior",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "let lastFormatted = '';\nfunction fixed(value) { return value.toFixed(2); }\nexport function formatInvoice(value) {\n  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error('nonfinite');\n  lastFormatted = fixed(value);\n  return lastFormatted;\n}\nexport function formatRows(rows) { return rows.map(row => formatInvoice(row.total)); }\n// Separate known bug, outside formatter refactor: this sorts numeric strings lexically.\nexport function sortAmounts(values) { return values.toSorted(); }\n"
    },

      "text": "export function formatAmount(value) {\n  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) throw new Error('nonfinite');\n  return value.toFixed(2);\n}\nexport function formatRows(rows) { return rows.map(row => formatAmount(row.total)); }\n// Separate known bug, outside formatter refactor: this sorts numeric strings lexically.\nexport function sortAmounts(values) { return values.toSorted(); }\n"

    {
      "path": "fixtures/cases/preserved-behavior/outputs/design/candidate-a.md",
      "bytes": 1559,
      "sha256": "aab5bcaa4c83698fcb842dfef20c1fe176ab07417f2ae67b4c56656dcd5b9063",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-preserved-behavior",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Sequential candidate A\n\n## Problem\n\nRemove the one-caller wrapper and duplicate display state while preserving literal strings and errors. The rename is internal and authorized by decision.md.\n\n## Usage\n\n`formatAmount(1.005)` returns `'1.00'`.\n`formatRows([{total:12.345}])` returns `['12.35']`.\n`exportAmounts([{total:0}])` returns `'0.00'`.\n\n## Shape\n\nKeep two modules. formatter.mjs exposes formatAmount(value), formatRows(rows), and the existing sortAmounts(values). Numbers and ordered row arrays remain the data structures. formatAmount returns its validated value.toFixed(2) directly. formatRows maps over the input. No module state exists. The exporter only joins strings. Sketch bodies throw Error('not implemented') before implementation.\n\nThis interface hides the display rule and its validation. It exposes no lifecycle steps. Foundational Thinking keeps the pin before the move. Model the Domain chooses a pure transform over duplicate mutable state.\n\n## Synthesis decision\n\nPending comparison.\n\n## Tradeoffs accepted\n\nWe accept the existing Number rounding behavior in exchange for an unchanged display contract.\n\n## Alternatives considered\n\nA Formatter instance can collect methods, but makes a stateless transform appear stateful. A configurable precision pipeline exposes an option no caller needs.\n\n## Open questions and risks\n\nCould callers outside the fixture depend on the former internal name? The authorized fixture scope is the only inspected usage boundary.\n\n## Next implementation step\n\nInline fixed and execute the unchanged pin.\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "fixtures/cases/preserved-behavior/outputs/design/synthesis.md",
      "bytes": 1487,
      "sha256": "567bb31b4016e989c33ad80572ef5f8c39d23b625831739d431cbb0b71056355",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-preserved-behavior",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Sequential design comparison\n\nRequested models and effort inherit-parent. Actual model and effort unknown. One local owner produced both alternatives. Independent runners and a fresh judge did not run because all slots were occupied. This is reduced architect and arena execution.\n\nThe rubric precedes implementation. Preserve literal behavior, remove redundant state, reduce calls to trace, complete all internal references, and add no unsupported options.\n\n| Criterion | A | B |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Literal behavior | Expected equivalent, must execute | Expected equivalent, must execute |\n| Redundant state | Removed | Retained in instance |\n| Calls to trace | Direct toFixed | Function, instance, method, toFixed |\n| Complete rename | Coordinated three-file wave | Same wave required |\n| Unsupported options | None | Future lifecycle without consumer |\n\nPick A. Red flag screening found no split ownership, temporal stages, hand-synced derived state, or pass-through in A. B retains pass-through and hidden state. Graft nothing from B. Read both candidates in full before this comparison. Interface depth is the same display knowledge hidden by fewer internal concepts in A. No independent judgment or model diversity is claimed.\n\nGround used how.md. Sketch produced both candidates and screened design-red-flags.md. Agree proceeds under existing authorization. Implement follows the pin. Scrap will retain the rejected trial diff and restore A if B fails to reduce reader load.\n"
    },

      "owner": "local-013-initial-preserved-behavior",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",

    {

      "owner": "local-013-initial-preserved-behavior",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "class Formatter {\n  lastFormatted = '';\n  amount(value) { throw new Error('not implemented'); }\n}\nexport function formatAmount(value) { throw new Error('not implemented'); }\nexport function formatRows(rows) { throw new Error('not implemented'); }\nexport function exportAmounts(rows) { throw new Error('not implemented'); }\n"

    {
      "path": "fixtures/cases/preserved-behavior/outputs/design/candidate-b.md",
      "bytes": 1440,
      "sha256": "2e7df43d9241516b3c30d66e074bc573037b25b94cb0f08a112c4d19590d05b3",
      "owner": "local-013-initial-preserved-behavior",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Sequential candidate B\n\n## Problem\n\nRemove the one-caller wrapper and duplicate display state while preserving literal strings and errors. The rename is internal and authorized by decision.md.\n\n## Usage\n\n`formatAmount(1.005)` returns `'1.00'`.\n`formatRows([{total:12.345}])` returns `['12.35']`.\n`exportAmounts([{total:0}])` returns `'0.00'`.\n\n## Shape\n\nKeep the two modules but create a private Formatter class with a lastFormatted field and an amount(value) method. A single module-owned instance handles calls. formatAmount delegates to that instance. formatRows still maps in order. Sketch class and functions throw Error('not implemented').\n\nThe interface hides rounding but also hides mutable state and a lifecycle with no consumer use. The same exported signatures require extra internal methods.\n\n## Synthesis decision\n\nPending comparison.\n\n## Tradeoffs accepted\n\nWe accept an instance and a method boundary for a place to collect future policies. This benefit is speculative.\n\n## Alternatives considered\n\nThe pure function avoids state and delegation, but has no object for hypothetical future configuration. A class exported to callers would expose lifecycle coordination and makes the interface larger.\n\n## Open questions and risks\n\nDoes any caller need formatter identity or configuration? None was found in inspected files.\n\n## Next implementation step\n\nBuild an isolated class trial only after the pure version stays green.\n"
    },

\"skills/architect/references/rationale-template.md\",\n      \"skills/architect/references/design-red-flags.md\"\n    ],\n    \"prompt\": \"Refactor a fixture invoice formatter by removing a one-caller wrapper and consolidating duplicated state. Preserve literal formatted outputs, empty inputs, rounding and errors. Capture the current outputs before edits. Rename its internal API and migrate code, string references, docs and back-references together. One fixture exposes an unrelated bug, which must stay separate. A speculative abstraction adds reader load and must be reverted. Permit local edits and execution but no external writes. Repeat with workers unavailable, execution denied and behavior coverage absent. Return the changed structure, held pin, equivalence proof, reader-load delta, shipped and reverted work.\",\n    \"requirements\": [\n      \"Read the full mode and eight-step Refactoring playbook, required leaves and routed companion contracts. Record steps before task-specific todos. Route large or cross-cutting work to figure-it-out and named redesigns that change behavior to Feature. Split newly discovered bugs or features out and deliver the structural change first against its pinned contract.\",\n      \"Use how to trace current behavior, then create and execute characterization tests, snapshots or an equivalence harness before moving structure. Write coverage first if absent. Type checks and lint are insufficient pins.\",\n      \"Name the missing domain structure and target module layout, types and call graph. Keep clear local boring code; new shape deletes branches or invalid states. Crossing a function boundary requires complete architect exploration with its runner prompts, rationale and candidate screening.\",\n      \"Subtract dead code, one-caller wrappers, redundant validators and orphan references before additions. Keep the smallest change that reaches the target and revert speculation.\",\n      \"Each small move keeps the original pin green. Migrate every API caller and delete the old API in the same wave without compatibility shims or parallel paths. Inspect actual files for rename usages in strings, prose and back-references. Delegate mechanical edits with the complete poteto prompt, exact revision, ownership and observed inherited or supported configured selection. Inspect artifacts and review the diff.\",\n      \"Verify unchanged behavior on the real artifact. Larger reshapes require old/new output diffs, baseline replay or a matching-surface smoke run through actual authorized controls. Retain outputs and failures.\",\n      \"Assess reduced layers to trace and state to hold. Revert a diff that lowers neither. Do not invent measured reader times.\",\n      \"Order verified subtraction, reshape and cleanup units. Rebase, commit and run complete Opening a PR only when authorized and available. Retain deferred dependencies and unperformed actions. Return all reply artifacts and no new behavior.\"\n    ],\n    \"expectedFull\": \"Observed authorized independent mechanical delegation returns inspected artifacts at the assigned revision. The before-edit pin and each subsequent step have matching literal output evidence, including errors and rounding. All callers and textual references move together and the old API disappears. Architecture exploration runs when triggered. The actual artifact has equivalence proof and a source-grounded reader-load reduction. Bug work remains separate and speculation is reverted. With explicit delivery authorization and complete dependencies, ordered green commits and an inspected PR establish delivery. Without external authorization, external delivery remains unperformed.\",\n    \"expectedReduced\": \"Unavailable workers permit authorized sequential edits and local equivalence checks with reduced capability disclosed. Independent delegation and parallel design remain unsatisfied. Denied writes allow only a source-grounded target shape and ordered proposal. No external authorization yields a checked local diff and delivery plan rather than a PR. Unknown actual models prove no diversity.\",\n    \"expectedBlocked\": \"Missing source or required leaves stops dependent work. No executable behavior pin blocks structure moves and unchanged-behavior claims. Denied equivalence execution or matching controls leaves behavior unverified. Missing independent operations block required independence rather than waive it. Missing Opening a PR stops PR-dependent work. Unauthorized writes, rebase, commits or PR creation stay unperformed.\",\n    \"forbiddenClaims\": [\n      \"Compilation, lint or structural validation proves unchanged behavior.\",\n      \"A pin captured after edits proves the pre-change contract.\",\n      \"New behavior or an unrelated bug fix belongs in the structural change.\",\n      \"Sequential roles prove independent delegation, parallel exploration or review.\",\n      \"The historical grok-4.7-xhigh-fast default proves current model availability, selection or diversity.\",\n      \"Requested or inherited models are actual observed models.\",\n      \"Caller migration is complete without checking strings, prose and back-references.\",\n      \"Compatibility shims or dual APIs satisfy the same-wave deletion gate.\",\n
